And he said, "If in hearing you hear the voice of the Lord your God and do the things pleasing before him and give ear to his commands and keep all his ordinances, every sickness that I brought upon the Egyptians I will not bring upon you, for I am the Lord who heals you."

**Exodus 15:27**

And they came to Elim, and there were there twelve springs of water and seventy trunks of palm trees, and they camped there by the waters.

### Chapter 16

**Exodus 16:1**

And they set out from Elim, and the whole assembly of the sons of Israel came into the wilderness of Sin, which is between Elim and Sinai, on the fifteenth day of the second month after they had come out from the land of Egypt.

**Exodus 16:2**

And the whole assembly of the sons of Israel grumbled against Moses and Aaron,

**Exodus 16:3**

and the sons of Israel said to them, "If only we had died, struck by the Lord in the land of Egypt, when we sat by the pots of meat and ate bread to fullness. But you have brought us out into this wilderness to kill this whole assembly with hunger."

**Exodus 16:4**

Then the Lord said to Moses, "Behold, I am raining bread for you from heaven, and the people will go out and gather what is needed for the day, day by day, so that I may test them whether they will walk in my law or not."

**Exodus 16:5**

"And it shall be on the sixth day that they will prepare what they bring in, and it will be double what they gather, day by day."

**Exodus 16:6**

And Moses and Aaron said to the whole assembly of the sons of Israel, "At evening you will know that the Lord has brought you out from the land of Egypt,"

**Exodus 16:7**

"and in the morning you will see the glory of the Lord, because he has heard your grumbling against God. And what are we, that you grumble against us?"

**Exodus 16:8**

And Moses said, "This will be when the Lord gives you meat to eat in the evening and bread in the morning to fullness, because the Lord has heard your grumbling that you grumble against us. And what are we? Your grumbling is not against us but against God."

**Exodus 16:9**

Then Moses said to Aaron, "Tell the whole assembly of the sons of Israel, Come near before God, for he has heard your grumbling."

**Exodus 16:10**

And when Aaron spoke to the whole assembly of the sons of Israel, they turned toward the wilderness, and the glory of the Lord appeared in a cloud.

**Exodus 16:11**

And the Lord spoke to Moses, saying,

**Exodus 16:12**

"I have heard the grumbling of the sons of Israel. Speak to them, saying, Toward evening you shall eat meat, and in the morning you shall be filled with bread, and you shall know that I am the Lord your God."

**Exodus 16:13**

And it happened at evening that quail came up and covered the camp, and in the morning, as the dew was settling around the camp,

**Exodus 16:14**

behold, over the face of the wilderness was a fine thing, like coriander seed, white like frost upon the earth.

**Exodus 16:15**

And when the sons of Israel saw it, they said one to another, "What is this?" for they did not know what it was. Then Moses said to them, "This is the bread that the Lord has given you to eat."

**Exodus 16:16**

"This is the word the Lord has commanded: Gather from it, each for those assigned to him, a gomor per head, according to the number of your persons. Each with those in your tent shall gather it."

**Exodus 16:17**

And the sons of Israel did so, and some gathered much and some little.

**Exodus 16:18**

And when they measured it by the gomor, the one who had much had nothing left over, and the one who had little had no lack. Each gathered according to those assigned to him.

**Exodus 16:19**

Then Moses said to them, "Let no one leave any of it until morning."

**Exodus 16:20**

But they did not listen to Moses, and some left from it until morning, and it bred worms and stank, and Moses was embittered against them.

**Exodus 16:21**

And they gathered it morning by morning, each according to what was needed for him. But when the sun grew hot, it melted.

**Exodus 16:22**

And it happened on the sixth day that they gathered what was needed double, two gomors for one person. Then all the rulers of the assembly came in and reported it to Moses.

**Exodus 16:23**

And Moses said to them, "This is the word the Lord spoke: Tomorrow is Sabbath, a holy rest to the Lord. Whatever you will bake, bake, and whatever you will boil, boil, and all that is left over leave stored up until morning."

**Exodus 16:24**

And they left some of it until morning, just as Moses commanded them, and it did not stink, nor did a worm appear in it.

**Exodus 16:25**

Then Moses said, "Eat it today, for today is Sabbath to the Lord. Today it will not be found in the field."

**Exodus 16:26**

"For six days you shall gather it, but on the seventh day is Sabbath, because it will not be on that day."

**Exodus 16:27**

And it happened on the seventh day that some of the people went out to gather, and they found none.

**Exodus 16:28**

Then the Lord said to Moses, "How long will you refuse to listen to my commands and my law?"

**Exodus 16:29**

"See, for the Lord has given you this day as the Sabbath. Because of this he has given you on the sixth day bread for two days. Let each stay in his houses. Let no one go out from his place on the seventh day."

**Exodus 16:30**

And the people Sabbathed on the seventh day.

**Exodus 16:31**

And the sons of Israel called its name Man. It was like white coriander seed, and its taste was like a cake in honey.

**Exodus 16:32**

Then Moses said, "This is the word the Lord commanded: Fill the gomor with Man for storage throughout your generations, so that they may see the bread that you ate in the wilderness when the Lord brought you out from the land of Egypt."

**Exodus 16:33**

And Moses said to Aaron, "Take one golden jar and put into it a full gomor of Man, and you shall place it before God for preservation throughout your generations,"

**Exodus 16:34**

just as the Lord commanded Moses, and Aaron placed it before the testimony for preservation.

**Exodus 16:35**

And the sons of Israel ate Man forty years, until they came into an inhabited land. They ate Man until they came to the region of Phoenicia.

**Exodus 16:36**

Now the gomor was the tenth part of three measures.

### Chapter 17

**Exodus 17:1**

And the whole assembly of the sons of Israel set out from the wilderness of Sin, by their encampments, according to the word of the Lord, and they camped in Raphidin. But there was no water for the people to drink.

**Exodus 17:2**

And the people reviled Moses, saying, "Give us water, so that we may drink." And Moses said to them, "Why do you revile me, and why do you test the Lord?"

**Exodus 17:3**

And there the people thirsted for water, and there the people grumbled against Moses, saying, "Why is this? Have you brought us up out from Egypt to kill us and our children and our livestock with thirst?"

**Exodus 17:4**

Then Moses cried out to the Lord, saying, "What shall I do with this people? A little more and they will stone me."

**Exodus 17:5**

And the Lord said to Moses, "Go on ahead of this people, and take with yourself some of the elders of the people. And the rod with which you struck the river, take in your hand and go."

**Exodus 17:6**

"Look, I am standing before you there on the rock in Horeb, and you shall strike the rock, and water will come out from it, and my people will drink." And Moses did so before the sons of Israel.

**Exodus 17:7**

And he called the name of that place Testing and Reviling, because of the reviling of the sons of Israel and because of their testing the Lord, saying, "Is the Lord among us or not?"

**Exodus 17:8**

Then Amalek came and fought with Israel in Raphidin.

**Exodus 17:9**

And Moses said to Joshua, "Choose for yourself strong men and go out and draw up in battle array against Amalek tomorrow. And look, I will stand on the top of the hill, and the rod of God will be in my hand."

**Exodus 17:10**

And Joshua did just as Moses told him, and he went out and drew up in battle array against Amalek. And Moses and Aaron and Hur went up to the top of the hill.

**Exodus 17:11**

And it happened that whenever Moses lifted up his hands, Israel prevailed, and whenever he let down his hands, Amalek prevailed.

**Exodus 17:12**

But Moses' hands were heavy, and taking a stone they put it under him, and he sat on it. And Aaron and Hur were supporting his hands, one from this side and one from that side, and Moses' hands became supported until the setting of the sun.

**Exodus 17:13**

And Joshua routed Amalek and all his people with the slaughter of the sword.

**Exodus 17:14**

Then the Lord said to Moses, "Write this as a memorial in a book, and put it into the ears of Joshua, for in wiping out I will wipe out the memorial of Amalek from under heaven."

**Exodus 17:15**

And Moses built an altar to the Lord and called its name, "The Lord is my refuge,"

**Exodus 17:16**

for with hidden hand the Lord wages war against Amalek from generation to generation.

### Chapter 18

**Exodus 18:1**

And Jethro, priest of Midian, the father-in-law of Moses, heard all that the Lord had done for Israel, his own people, for the Lord had brought Israel out from Egypt.

**Exodus 18:2**

And Jethro, the father-in-law of Moses, took Zipporah, the wife of Moses, after her sending away.

**Exodus 18:3**

and her two sons. The name of one was Gersam, saying, "I was a resident alien in a foreign land,"

**Exodus 18:4**

and the name of the second was Eliezer, saying, "For the God of my father is my helper, and he rescued me from the hand of Pharaoh."

**Exodus 18:5**

And Jethro, the father-in-law of Moses, and his sons and wife, came out to Moses into the wilderness where he had camped at the mountain of God.

**Exodus 18:6**

And word was reported to Moses, saying, "Look, your father-in-law Jethro is coming to you, and your wife and your two sons with him."

**Exodus 18:7**

Then Moses went out to meet his father-in-law and bowed down to him and kissed him, and they greeted one another and brought him into the tent.

**Exodus 18:8**

And Moses recounted to his father-in-law all that the Lord had done to Pharaoh and the Egyptians for Israel's sake, and all the hardship that had come upon them on the way, and that the Lord had rescued them from the hand of Pharaoh and from the hand of the Egyptians.

**Exodus 18:9**

And Jethro was amazed at all the good things that the Lord had done for them, that he had rescued them from the hand of the Egyptians and from the hand of Pharaoh.

**Exodus 18:10**

And Jethro said, "Blessed be the Lord, because he has rescued his people from the hand of the Egyptians and from the hand of Pharaoh."

**Exodus 18:11**

"Now I know that the Lord is great above all the gods, because in this matter they acted arrogantly against them."

**Exodus 18:12**

And Jethro, the father-in-law of Moses, took whole burnt offerings and sacrifices to God. And Aaron and all the elders of Israel came to eat bread with the father-in-law of Moses before God.

**Exodus 18:13**

And it happened on the next day that Moses sat to judge the people, and all the people stood by Moses from morning until evening.

**Exodus 18:14**

And when Jethro saw all that Moses was doing for the people, he said, "What is this that you are doing for the people? Why do you sit alone while all the people stand by you from morning until evening?"

**Exodus 18:15**

And Moses said to his father-in-law, "Because the people come to me to seek judgment from God."

**Exodus 18:16**

"For whenever they have a dispute and come to me, I decide each one and instruct them in the ordinances of God and his law."

**Exodus 18:17**

Then the father-in-law of Moses said to him, "This thing that you are doing is not right."

**Exodus 18:18**

"With ruin you will wear away, with unbearable exhaustion, both you and all this people that is with you. This thing is too heavy for you. You will not be able to do it alone."

**Exodus 18:19**

"Now then, listen to me, and I will advise you, and God will be with you. Be for the people in the things toward God, and you shall bring their words to God."

**Exodus 18:20**

"And you shall testify to them the ordinances of God and his law, and you shall make known to them the ways in which they shall walk and the works that they shall do."

**Exodus 18:21**

"And you, select for yourself from all the people able men, God-fearing men, righteous men, hating arrogance, and you shall appoint them over them as commanders of thousands and hundreds and fifties and tens."

**Exodus 18:22**

"And they shall judge the people at every hour. The over-large matter they shall bring to you, but the small cases they shall judge themselves, and they shall lighten the load from you and help you."

**Exodus 18:23**

"If you do this thing, God will strengthen you, and you will be able to stand, and all this people will come to its own place with peace."

**Exodus 18:24**

And Moses listened to the voice of his father-in-law and did all that he said to him.

**Exodus 18:25**

And Moses chose able men from all Israel and made them over the people as commanders of thousands and hundreds and fifties and tens.

**Exodus 18:26**

And they judged the people at every hour. Every over-large matter they brought to Moses, but every light matter they judged themselves.

**Exodus 18:27**

Then Moses sent away his father-in-law, and he went to his own land.

### Chapter 19

**Exodus 19:1**

And in the third month of the departure of the sons of Israel from the land of Egypt, on this day they came into the wilderness of Sinai.

**Exodus 19:2**

And they set out from Raphidin and came into the wilderness of Sinai, and Israel camped there opposite the mountain.

**Exodus 19:3**

And Moses went up to the mountain of God, and God called to him from the mountain, saying, "These things you shall say to the house of Jacob, and you shall announce to the sons of Israel:"

**Exodus 19:4**

"You yourselves have seen what I did to the Egyptians, and I took you up as on wings of eagles, and I brought you near to myself."

**Exodus 19:5**

"And now, if in hearing you hear my voice and keep my covenant, you shall be to me a people of special possession from among all the nations, for the whole earth is mine."

**Exodus 19:6**

"And you shall be to me a royal priesthood and a holy nation. These are the words you shall speak to the sons of Israel."

**Exodus 19:7**

Then Moses came and called the elders of the people and set before them all these words that God had commanded him.

**Exodus 19:8**

And all the people answered with one accord and said, "All that God has spoken, we will do and we will hear." And Moses carried the words of the people up to God.

**Exodus 19:9**

Then the Lord said to Moses, "Look, I am coming to you in a pillar of cloud, so that the people may hear me speaking with you and may trust you forever." And Moses reported the words of the people to the Lord.

**Exodus 19:10**

And the Lord said to Moses, "Go down and solemnly warn the people, and purify them today and tomorrow, and let them wash their garments,"

**Exodus 19:11**

"and let them be ready for the third day, for on the third day the Lord will come down upon Mount Sinai before all the people."

**Exodus 19:12**

"And you shall mark off the people all around, saying, Take heed for yourselves not to go up on the mountain or touch any part of it. Everyone who touches the mountain shall surely die."

**Exodus 19:13**

"No hand shall touch him, for he shall certainly be stoned with stones or shot through with a missile. Whether beast or man, he shall not live. When the voices and the trumpets and the cloud depart from the mountain, they may come up on the mountain."

**Exodus 19:14**

Then Moses went down from the mountain to the people and sanctified them, and they washed their garments.

**Exodus 19:15**

And he said to the people, "Be ready for three days. Do not come near a woman."

**Exodus 19:16**

And it happened on the third day, when morning came, that there were voices and lightnings and a gloomy cloud on Mount Sinai. The sound of the trumpet rang out loudly, and all the people in the camp were terrified.

**Exodus 19:17**

And Moses brought the people out from the camp to meet God, and they stood beneath the mountain.

**Exodus 19:18**

And Mount Sinai smoked all over because God had come down upon it in fire, and the smoke went up like the smoke of a furnace, and all the people were exceedingly astonished.

**Exodus 19:19**

And the sounds of the trumpet kept advancing, growing much stronger. Moses was speaking, and God answered him with a voice.

**Exodus 19:20**

And the Lord came down on Mount Sinai, on the top of the mountain, and the Lord called Moses to the top of the mountain, and Moses went up.

**Exodus 19:21**

And God said to Moses, saying, "Go down and solemnly warn the people lest they draw near to God to gaze, and a multitude from among them fall."

**Exodus 19:22**

"And let the priests who draw near to the Lord God sanctify themselves, lest the Lord break out against some of them."

**Exodus 19:23**

And Moses said to God, "The people will not be able to come up to Mount Sinai, for you yourself solemnly warned us, saying, Mark off the mountain and sanctify it."

**Exodus 19:24**

And the Lord said to him, "Go, go down, and then come up, you and Aaron with you. But let not the priests and the people force their way to come up to God, lest the Lord destroy some of them."

**Exodus 19:25**

Then Moses went down to the people and spoke to them.

### Chapter 20

**Exodus 20:1**

And the Lord spoke all these words, saying,

**Exodus 20:2**

"I am the Lord your God, who brought you out from the land of Egypt, out of the house of slavery."

**Exodus 20:3**

"No other gods shall be yours besides me."

**Exodus 20:4**

"You shall not make for yourself an idol, nor any likeness of whatever is in heaven above and whatever is in the earth below and whatever is in the waters beneath the earth."

**Exodus 20:5**

"You shall not bow down to them, nor indeed serve them, for I am the Lord your God, a jealous God, repaying fathers' sins upon children to the third and fourth generation of those who hate me,"

**Exodus 20:6**

"and showing mercy into thousands for those who love me and keep my commandments."

**Exodus 20:7**

"You shall not take the name of the Lord your God upon emptiness, for the Lord will certainly not clear the one who takes his name upon emptiness."

**Exodus 20:8**

"Remember the day of the Sabbaths, to keep it holy."

**Exodus 20:9**

"For six days you shall work and do all your works,"

**Exodus 20:10**

"but the seventh day is Sabbath to the Lord your God. On it you shall do no work, you and your son and your daughter, your male servant and your female servant, your ox and your pack animal and every beast of yours and the newcomer staying among you."

**Exodus 20:11**

"For in six days the Lord made heaven and earth and the sea and everything in them, and he rested on the seventh day. Therefore the Lord blessed the seventh day and sanctified it."

**Exodus 20:12**

"Honor your father and your mother, so that it may go well for you and so that you may become long-lived on the good land that the Lord your God is giving you."

**Exodus 20:13**

"You shall not commit adultery."

**Exodus 20:14**

"You shall not steal."

**Exodus 20:15**

"You shall not murder."

**Exodus 20:16**

"You shall not bear false witness against your neighbor, false testimony."

**Exodus 20:17**

"You shall not desire your neighbor's wife. You shall not desire your neighbor's house, nor his field, nor his male servant, nor his female servant, nor his ox, nor his pack animal, nor any beast of his, nor whatever belongs to your neighbor."

**Exodus 20:18**

And all the people were seeing the voice and the torches and the sound of the trumpet and the mountain smoking. And all the people were afraid and stood far off,

**Exodus 20:19**

and they said to Moses, "You speak to us, and let not God speak to us, lest we die."

**Exodus 20:20**

And Moses said to them, "Take courage, for God has come for the sake of testing you, so that his fear may be among you, so that you may not sin."

**Exodus 20:21**

And the people stood far off, but Moses entered into the gloom where God was.

**Exodus 20:22**

Then the Lord said to Moses, "These things you shall say to the house of Jacob, and you shall announce to the sons of Israel: You yourselves have seen that from heaven I have spoken to you."

**Exodus 20:23**

"You shall not make for yourselves gods of silver, and gods of gold you shall not make for yourselves."

**Exodus 20:24**

"You shall make me an altar of earth, and upon it you shall sacrifice your whole burnt offerings and your offerings of salvation, your sheep and your calves. In every place where I name my name, there I will come to you and bless you."

**Exodus 20:25**

"But if you make me an altar of stones, you shall not build them cut, for you have laid your tool upon them, and they have been defiled."

**Exodus 20:26**

"And you shall not go up by steps to my altar, so that you do not uncover your indecency upon it."

### Chapter 21

**Exodus 21:1**

And these are the ordinances that you shall set before them.

**Exodus 21:2**

If you buy a Hebrew servant, six years he shall serve you, and in the seventh year he shall go out free for nothing.

**Exodus 21:3**

If he comes in alone, he shall also go out alone. But if his wife comes in with him, his wife shall also go out with him.

**Exodus 21:4**

But if his master gives him a wife and she bears him sons or daughters, the wife and the children shall belong to his master, and he alone shall go out.

**Exodus 21:5**

But if the servant answers and says, "I have loved my master and my wife and my children. I will not run away free,"

**Exodus 21:6**

then his master shall bring him to the judgment-seat of God. Then he shall bring him to the door, to the doorpost, and his master shall pierce his ear with an awl, and he shall serve him forever.

**Exodus 21:7**

If someone sells his own daughter as a household servant, she shall not go out as the female servants go out.

**Exodus 21:8**

If she does not please her master, to whom she had been promised, he shall let her be redeemed. He has no authority to sell her to a foreign nation, because he has broken faith with her.

**Exodus 21:9**

But if he promises her to his son, he shall treat her according to the right of daughters.

**Exodus 21:10**

But if he takes another woman for himself, he shall not deprive her of what is needed, or of clothing, or of her companionship.

**Exodus 21:11**

But if he does not do these three things for her, she shall go out free, without money.

**Exodus 21:12**

If someone strikes a person and he dies, let him certainly be put to death.

**Exodus 21:13**

But if he did not do it willingly, and God delivered him into his hands, I will give you a place where the killer may flee.

**Exodus 21:14**

But if someone attacks his neighbor to kill him with deceit, and he flees to my altar, you shall take him from there to put him to death.

**Exodus 21:15**

Whoever strikes his father or his mother, let him certainly be put to death.

**Exodus 21:16**

Whoever reviles his father or his mother shall surely die.

**Exodus 21:17**

Whoever steals one of the sons of Israel, and after overpowering him sells him, and he is found in his hand, let him certainly be put to death.

**Exodus 21:18**

If two men revile each other and one strikes his neighbor with a stone or with his fist and he does not die but is laid in bed,

**Exodus 21:19**

if the man rises and walks about outside on his staff, the one who struck him shall be clear. Yet he shall pay for his idleness and for his healing.

**Exodus 21:20**

If someone strikes his male servant or his female servant with a rod and the servant dies under his hands, let justice be exacted.

**Exodus 21:21**

But if the servant lives a day or two, he shall not be avenged, for he is his money.

**Exodus 21:22**

If two men fight and strike a pregnant woman, and her child comes out not yet formed, he shall pay a penalty according as the woman's husband lays upon him. He shall pay with assessment.

**Exodus 21:23**

But if it is formed, he shall give life for life,

**Exodus 21:24**

eye for eye, tooth for tooth, hand for hand, foot for foot,

**Exodus 21:25**

burn for burn, wound for wound, bruise for bruise.

**Exodus 21:26**

If someone strikes the eye of his servant or the eye of his female servant and blinds it, he shall send them out free for their eye.

**Exodus 21:27**

And if he knocks out the tooth of his servant or the tooth of his female servant, he shall send them out free for their tooth.

**Exodus 21:28**

If a bull gores a man or a woman and they die, the bull shall be stoned with stones, and its flesh shall not be eaten. But the bull's owner shall be clear.

**Exodus 21:29**

But if the bull has been a gorer since yesterday and the third day, and they have testified to its owner and he has not confined it, and it kills a man or a woman, the bull shall be stoned, and its owner shall also die.

**Exodus 21:30**

But if a ransom is laid upon him, he shall give a ransom for his life, whatever they lay upon him.

**Exodus 21:31**

If it gores a son or a daughter, they shall do to him according to this ordinance.

**Exodus 21:32**

But if the bull gores a male servant or a female servant, he shall give thirty silver didrachms to their master, and the bull shall be stoned.

**Exodus 21:33**

If someone opens a pit or quarries a pit and does not cover it, and an ox or a donkey falls in there,

**Exodus 21:34**

the owner of the pit shall make compensation. He shall give silver to their owner, and the dead animal shall be his.

**Exodus 21:35**

If one person's bull gores the bull of his neighbor and it dies, they shall sell the living bull and divide its silver, and they shall divide the dead one.

**Exodus 21:36**

But if it is known that the bull has been a gorer since yesterday and the third day, and testimony had been made to its owner, and he did not confine it, he shall repay bull for bull, and the dead one shall be his.

**Exodus 21:37**

If someone steals an ox or a sheep and slaughters it or sells it, he shall repay five oxen for the ox and four sheep for the sheep.

### Chapter 22

**Exodus 22:1**

If the thief is found in the breach and is struck and dies, there is no bloodguilt for him.

**Exodus 22:2**

But if the sun rises on him, there is guilt. He shall die in return. But if he has nothing, let him be sold for what was stolen.

**Exodus 22:3**

If the stolen thing is found alive in his hand, whether donkey or sheep, he shall repay them double.

**Exodus 22:4**

If someone lets a field or vineyard be grazed and lets his beast graze another field, he shall repay from his own field according to its produce. But if it grazes the whole field, he shall repay the best of his own field and the best of his vineyard.

**Exodus 22:5**

If fire goes out and finds thorns and sets aflame a threshing floor or sheaves or a field, the one who kindled the fire shall make repayment.

**Exodus 22:6**

If someone gives his neighbor silver or goods to keep, and it is stolen from the man's house, if the thief is found, he shall repay double.

**Exodus 22:7**

But if the thief is not found, the master of the house shall come before God and swear that he has surely not acted wickedly against any part of his neighbor's deposit.

**Exodus 22:8**

In every matter of wrongdoing, concerning an ox and a beast of burden and a sheep and a garment and every alleged loss, whatever it may be, the judgment of both shall come before God, and the one convicted through God shall repay double to his neighbor.

**Exodus 22:9**

If someone gives his neighbor a beast of burden or an ox or a sheep or any livestock to keep, and it is injured or dies or is taken captive, and no one knows,

**Exodus 22:10**

an oath of God shall be between both of them, that he surely has not acted wickedly against any part of his neighbor's deposit. So its owner shall accept it, and he shall not repay.

**Exodus 22:11**

But if it is stolen from him, he shall repay its owner.

**Exodus 22:12**

But if it becomes beast-torn, he shall bring it as evidence of the prey, and he shall not repay.

**Exodus 22:13**

If someone borrows from his neighbor, and it is injured or dies or is taken captive, and its owner is not with it, he shall repay.

**Exodus 22:14**

But if the owner is with it, he shall not repay. But if it is hired, it shall stand for its hire.

**Exodus 22:15**

If someone entices an unbetrothed virgin and lies with her, he shall surely pay the bride-price and take her as wife for himself.

**Exodus 22:16**

But if her father utterly refuses and does not wish to give her to him as wife, he shall pay silver to her father according to the bride-price of virgins.

**Exodus 22:17**

You shall not preserve sorcerers alive.

**Exodus 22:18**

Everyone who lies with an animal, you shall certainly put them to death.

**Exodus 22:19**

The one sacrificing to gods shall be destroyed by death, except to the Lord alone.

**Exodus 22:20**

You shall not mistreat a newcomer, nor oppress him, for you were newcomers in the land of Egypt.

**Exodus 22:21**

You shall mistreat no widow or orphan.

**Exodus 22:22**

But if by evil-treatment you mistreat them, and crying out they cry to me, I will surely hear their voice.

**Exodus 22:23**

And I will become angry with wrath and kill you with the sword, and your wives shall be widows and your children orphans.

**Exodus 22:24**

If you lend silver to your poor brother beside you, you shall not press him hard, nor lay interest upon him.

**Exodus 22:25**

If you take your neighbor's garment as a pledge, you shall give it back to him before sunset.

**Exodus 22:26**

For this alone is his covering; this is the garment of his nakedness. In what shall he sleep? So if he cries out to me, I will hear him, for I am merciful.

**Exodus 22:27**

You shall not revile gods, and you shall not speak evil of a ruler of your people.

**Exodus 22:28**

You shall not delay the firstfruits of your threshing floor and your winepress. You shall give me the firstborn of your sons.

**Exodus 22:29**

So you shall do with your ox and your sheep and your beast of burden. Seven days it shall be under its mother, and on the eighth day you shall give it to me.

**Exodus 22:30**

And you shall be holy men to me, and you shall not eat flesh torn by beasts; you shall throw it to the dog.

### Chapter 23

**Exodus 23:1**

You shall not accept a vain report. You shall not join with the unjust to become an unjust witness.

**Exodus 23:2**

You shall not be with the many for evil. You shall not side with a crowd to turn aside with the many so as to bend justice.

**Exodus 23:3**

And you shall not spare a poor man in judgment.

**Exodus 23:4**

If you meet your enemy's ox or his beast of burden wandering, you shall surely turn it back and return it to him.

**Exodus 23:5**

If you see your enemy's beast of burden fallen under its load, you shall not pass it by, but you shall help raise it with him.

**Exodus 23:6**

You shall not pervert the judgment of a poor man in his case.

**Exodus 23:7**

Keep far from every unjust word. You shall not kill the innocent and the righteous, and you shall not justify the ungodly for gifts.

**Exodus 23:8**

You shall not take gifts, for gifts blind the eyes of those who see and destroy just words.

**Exodus 23:9**

You shall not oppress a newcomer, for you know the soul of the newcomer, because you were newcomers in the land of Egypt.

**Exodus 23:10**

Six years you shall sow your land and gather its produce.

**Exodus 23:11**

But in the seventh you shall make a release and let it rest, and the poor of your nation shall eat, and what they leave the wild beasts shall eat. So you shall do with your vineyard and your oliveyard.

**Exodus 23:12**

Six days you shall do your works, but on the seventh day there shall be rest, so that your ox and your beast of burden may rest, and the son of your female servant and the newcomer may be refreshed.

**Exodus 23:13**

Guard all that I have said to you, and you shall not mention the name of other gods, nor shall it be heard from your mouth.

**Exodus 23:14**

Three times of the year celebrate a feast to me.

**Exodus 23:15**

Keep the feast of unleavened bread. Seven days you shall eat unleavened bread, just as I commanded you, at the time of the month of new grain, for in it you came out from Egypt. You shall not appear before me empty.

**Exodus 23:16**

And you shall keep the feast of harvest of the first produce of your labors, whatever you sow in your field, and the feast of completion at the going out of the year, in the gathering of your labors from your field.

**Exodus 23:17**

Three times of the year every male of yours shall appear before the Lord your God.

**Exodus 23:18**

For when I cast out nations from before you and widen your borders, you shall not sacrifice the blood of my sacrifice with leaven, nor shall the fat of my feast lie down until morning.

**Exodus 23:19**

You shall bring the firstfruits of the first produce of your land into the house of the Lord your God. You shall not boil a lamb in its mother's milk.

**Exodus 23:20**

And behold, I send my messenger before your face to keep you on the way, so that he may bring you into the land that I have prepared for you.

**Exodus 23:21**

Pay attention to yourself and listen to him, and do not disobey him, for he will never yield to you, because my name is upon him.

**Exodus 23:22**

If you truly hear my voice and do all that I command you and keep my covenant, you shall be for me a people of special possession out of all nations, for all the earth is mine. And you shall be for me a royal priesthood and a holy nation. These are the words you shall speak to the sons of Israel. If you truly hear my voice and do all that I say to you, I will be hostile to your enemies and oppose those who oppose you.

**Exodus 23:23**

For my messenger shall go leading you and bring you to the Amorite and Hittite and Perizzite and Canaanite and Girgashite and Hivite and Jebusite, and I will wipe them out.

**Exodus 23:24**

You shall not worship their gods, nor serve them. You shall not do according to their works, but by pulling down you shall pull down, and by breaking you shall break their pillars.

**Exodus 23:25**

And you shall serve the Lord your God, and I will bless your bread and your wine and your water, and I will turn sickness away from you.

**Exodus 23:26**

None shall be barren or sterile in your land. I will fill up the number of your days.

**Exodus 23:27**

And I will send fear ahead of you and throw into confusion all the nations among whom you enter, and I will make all your adversaries flee.

**Exodus 23:28**

And I will send hornets ahead of you, and they shall drive out the Amorites and the Hivites and the Canaanites and the Hittites from before you.

**Exodus 23:29**

I will not drive them out in one year, lest the land become desolate and the beasts of the earth multiply against you.

**Exodus 23:30**

Little by little I will drive them out from before you, until you increase and inherit the land.

**Exodus 23:31**

And I will set your borders from the Red Sea to the Sea of the Philistines, and from the wilderness to the great river Euphrates. And I will give the inhabitants of the land into your hands, and I will drive them out from before you.

**Exodus 23:32**

You shall make no covenant with them or with their gods.

**Exodus 23:33**

They shall not dwell in your land, lest they make you sin against me. For if you serve their gods, these will be a stumbling block to you.

### Chapter 24

**Exodus 24:1**

And to Moses he said, "Come up to the Lord, you and Aaron and Nadab and Abihu and seventy of the elders of Israel, and worship from far off."

**Exodus 24:2**

And Moses alone shall come near to God. But they shall not come near, and the people shall not come up with them.

**Exodus 24:3**

And Moses went in and related to the people all the words of God and the ordinances. And all the people answered with one voice, saying, "All the words that the Lord has spoken we will do and we will hear."

**Exodus 24:4**

And Moses wrote all the words of the Lord. Rising early in the morning, Moses built an altar under the mountain, and twelve stones for the twelve tribes of Israel.

**Exodus 24:5**

And he sent the young men of the sons of Israel, and they offered whole burnt offerings and sacrificed calves as offerings of salvation to God.

**Exodus 24:6**

And Moses took half the blood and poured it into bowls, and half the blood he poured out upon the altar.

**Exodus 24:7**

And taking the book of the covenant, he read it in the hearing of the people, and they said, "All that the Lord has spoken we will do and we will hear."

**Exodus 24:8**

And Moses took the blood and splashed it on the people and said, "Behold, the blood of the covenant that the Lord has made with you concerning all these words."

**Exodus 24:9**

Then Moses and Aaron and Nadab and Abihu and seventy from the council of Israel went up.

**Exodus 24:10**

And they saw the place where the God of Israel had stood. And under his feet was like work of sapphire brick, and like the appearance of the firmament of heaven in purity.

**Exodus 24:11**

And of the chosen ones of Israel not even one was missing. And they appeared in the place of God, and they ate and drank.

**Exodus 24:12**

And the Lord said to Moses, "Come up to me on the mountain and stay there, and I will give you the stone tablets, the law and the commandments that I wrote to give them law."

**Exodus 24:13**

And Moses rose, and Joshua who stood beside him, and they went up into the mountain of God.

**Exodus 24:14**

And to the elders they said, "Stay still there until we return to you. And look, Aaron and Hur are with you. If any matter of judgment happens to anyone, let them go to them."

**Exodus 24:15**

And Moses and Joshua went up into the mountain, and the cloud covered the mountain.

**Exodus 24:16**

And the glory of God came down upon Mount Sinai, and the cloud covered it six days. And on the seventh day the Lord called Moses from the midst of the cloud.

**Exodus 24:17**

And the appearance of the glory of the Lord was like burning fire on the top of the mountain before the sons of Israel.

**Exodus 24:18**

And Moses entered into the midst of the cloud and went up into the mountain. And he was there on the mountain forty days and forty nights.

### Chapter 25

**Exodus 25:1**

And the Lord spoke to Moses, saying,

**Exodus 25:2**

"Tell the sons of Israel to take contributions for me. From everyone whose heart deems it good, you shall take my contributions."

**Exodus 25:3**

And this is the contribution that you shall take from them: gold and silver and bronze,

**Exodus 25:4**

and blue and purple and doubled scarlet and spun fine linen and goat hair,

**Exodus 25:5**

and rams' skins dyed red and blue skins and incorruptible wood,

**Exodus 25:7**

and sardius stones and stones for engraving, for the ephod and the robe.

**Exodus 25:8**

And you shall make me a holy place, and I will be seen among you.

**Exodus 25:9**

And you shall make them for me according to all that I show you on the mountain: the pattern of the tent and the pattern of all its furnishings. So you shall make them.

**Exodus 25:10**

And you shall make an ark of testimony of incorruptible wood: two and a half cubits its length, one and a half cubits its breadth, and one and a half cubits its height.

**Exodus 25:11**

And you shall overlay it with pure gold. Inside and outside you shall overlay it, and you shall make for it twisted golden moldings all around.

**Exodus 25:12**

And you shall cast for it four golden rings and place them on its four sides, two rings on one side and two rings on the second side.

**Exodus 25:13**

And you shall make carrying-poles of incorruptible wood and overlay them with gold.

**Exodus 25:14**

And you shall put the poles into the rings on the sides of the ark, to carry the ark by them.

**Exodus 25:15**

The poles shall stay fixed in the rings of the ark.

**Exodus 25:16**

And you shall put into the ark the testimonies that I shall give you.

**Exodus 25:17**

And you shall make a propitiatory lid of pure gold: two and a half cubits its length, and one and a half cubits its breadth.

**Exodus 25:18**

And you shall make two cherubim of beaten gold, and you shall place them on both ends of the propitiatory.

**Exodus 25:19**

One cherub shall be made on this end and one cherub on the second end of the propitiatory. You shall make the two cherubim on the two ends.

**Exodus 25:20**

And the cherubim shall be stretching out their wings above, overshadowing the propitiatory with their wings, and their faces shall be toward one another. The faces of the cherubim shall be toward the propitiatory.

**Exodus 25:21**

And you shall place the propitiatory on top of the ark, and into the ark you shall put the testimonies that I shall give you.

**Exodus 25:22**

And I will make myself known to you there, and I will speak to you from above the propitiatory, from between the two cherubim that are on the ark of testimony, according to all that I shall command you for the sons of Israel.

**Exodus 25:23**

And you shall make a table of pure gold: two cubits its length, one cubit its breadth, and one and a half cubits its height.

**Exodus 25:24**

And you shall make for it twisted golden moldings all around.

**Exodus 25:25**

And you shall make for it a rim a handbreadth around it, and you shall make a twisted molding around the rim.

**Exodus 25:26**

And you shall make four golden rings and place the rings on the four parts of its feet.

**Exodus 25:27**

The rings shall be under the rim, as holders for the carrying-poles, so that the table may be carried by them.

**Exodus 25:28**

And you shall make the poles of incorruptible wood and overlay them with pure gold, and the table shall be carried by them.

**Exodus 25:29**

And you shall make its dishes and censers and libation bowls and cups with which you shall pour offerings. You shall make them of pure gold.

**Exodus 25:30**

And you shall set on the table loaves before my face continually.

**Exodus 25:31**

And you shall make a lampstand of pure gold. Beaten work shall you make the lampstand: its shaft and its branches, its bowls and knobs and lilies shall be from it.

**Exodus 25:32**

Six branches shall come out from its sides: three branches of the lampstand from one side and three branches of the lampstand from the second side.

**Exodus 25:33**

Three almond-shaped bowls on one branch, with knob and lily. So also on the six branches that come out from the lampstand.

**Exodus 25:34**

And on the lampstand four almond-shaped bowls, with its knobs and its lilies.

**Exodus 25:35**

A knob under two branches from it, and a knob under four branches from it, so also for the six branches that come out from the lampstand.

**Exodus 25:36**

Their knobs and their branches shall be from it. All shall be one beaten piece of pure gold.

**Exodus 25:37**

And you shall make its seven lamps. And you shall set up the lamps, and they shall shine from the one front.

**Exodus 25:38**

And you shall make its snuffers and its trays of pure gold.

**Exodus 25:39**

All these utensils shall be one talent of pure gold.

**Exodus 25:40**

See that you make them according to the pattern shown you on the mountain.

### Chapter 26

**Exodus 26:1**

And the tent you shall make with ten curtains of woven fine linen and blue and purple and spun scarlet. You shall make them with cherubim, woven work.

**Exodus 26:2**

The length of one curtain shall be twenty-eight cubits, and the breadth of one curtain four cubits. One measure shall belong to all the curtains.

**Exodus 26:3**

Five curtains shall be joined one to another, and five curtains shall be held together one to another.

**Exodus 26:4**

And you shall make blue loops on the edge of one curtain on one side for the coupling, and so you shall make on the edge of the outer curtain for the second coupling.

**Exodus 26:5**

Fifty loops you shall make on the one curtain, and fifty loops on the part of the curtain at the coupling of the second, matching one another.

**Exodus 26:6**

And you shall make fifty gold clasps and join the curtains one to another with the clasps, and the tent shall be one.

**Exodus 26:7**

And you shall make goat-hair coverings as a covering over the tent. Eleven coverings you shall make.

**Exodus 26:8**

The length of one covering shall be thirty cubits, and the breadth of one covering four cubits. One measure shall belong to the eleven coverings.

**Exodus 26:9**

And you shall join the five coverings together and the six coverings together, and you shall fold the sixth covering over at the front of the tent.

**Exodus 26:10**

And you shall make fifty loops on the edge of the one covering that is in the middle at the joining, and fifty loops on the edge of the second covering that joins it.

**Exodus 26:11**

And you shall make fifty bronze clasps, and you shall join the clasps through the loops and join the coverings, and it shall be one.

**Exodus 26:12**

And the extra part in the coverings of the tent, the half-covering that remains, you shall let hang over the back of the tent.

**Exodus 26:13**

A cubit on this side and a cubit on that side, from the excess in the length of the tent coverings, shall hang over the sides of the tent on this side and that side to cover it.

**Exodus 26:14**

And you shall make for the tent a covering of red-dyed rams' skins, and coverings of blue skins above.

**Exodus 26:15**

And you shall make uprights for the tent of incorruptible wood.

**Exodus 26:16**

Ten cubits shall you make one upright, and one and a half cubits the breadth of one upright.

**Exodus 26:17**

Two tenons for each upright, corresponding one to another. So you shall do for all the uprights of the tent.

**Exodus 26:18**

And you shall make the uprights for the tent, twenty uprights on the side toward the north.

**Exodus 26:19**

And you shall make forty silver bases for the twenty uprights, two bases for one upright on its two sides and two bases for the next upright on its two sides.

**Exodus 26:20**

And on the second side, the side toward the south, twenty uprights.

**Exodus 26:21**

And their forty silver bases, two bases for one upright on its two sides and two bases for the next upright on its two sides.

**Exodus 26:22**

And at the back of the tent, at the side toward the sea, you shall make six uprights.

**Exodus 26:23**

And you shall make two uprights for the corners of the tent at the back.

**Exodus 26:24**

And they shall be equal below, and together they shall be equal up to the tops for one joining. So you shall make them for both, for the two corners.

**Exodus 26:25**

And there shall be eight uprights, and their silver bases sixteen: two bases for one upright on its two sides and two bases for the next upright.

**Exodus 26:26**

And you shall make bars of incorruptible wood, five for the uprights on one side of the tent,

**Exodus 26:27**

and five bars for the uprights on the second side of the tent, and five bars for the hindmost uprights on the side of the tent toward the sea.

**Exodus 26:28**

And the middle bar, in the midst of the uprights, shall pass through from one side to the other.

**Exodus 26:29**

And you shall overlay the uprights with gold, and you shall make gold rings into which you shall bring in the bars, and you shall overlay the bars with gold.

**Exodus 26:30**

And you shall set up the tent according to the pattern shown you on the mountain.

**Exodus 26:31**

And you shall make a curtain of blue and purple and spun scarlet and woven fine linen. You shall make it with cherubim, woven work.

**Exodus 26:32**

And you shall place it on four incorruptible posts overlaid with gold, their capitals gold, and their four bases silver.

**Exodus 26:33**

And you shall put the curtain on the posts, and within the curtain you shall bring in there the ark of testimony. And the curtain shall divide for you between the Holy Place and the Holy of Holies.

**Exodus 26:34**

And with the curtain you shall cover the ark of testimony in the Holy of Holies.

**Exodus 26:35**

And you shall place the table outside the curtain, and the lampstand opposite the table on the south side of the tent. And you shall put the table on the north side of the tent.

**Exodus 26:36**

And you shall make a screen for the entrance of the tent, of blue and purple and spun scarlet and woven fine linen, embroidered work.

**Exodus 26:37**

And you shall make for the screen five posts and overlay them with gold, and their capitals gold, and you shall cast for them five bronze bases.

### Chapter 27

**Exodus 27:1**

And you shall make the altar of incorruptible wood, five cubits its length and five cubits its breadth. The altar shall be square, and its height three cubits.

**Exodus 27:2**

And you shall make the horns upon its four corners. The horns shall be from it, and you shall cover them with bronze.

**Exodus 27:3**

And you shall make a rim for the altar, and its pans and its bowls and its flesh-hooks and its fire-pan. All its utensils you shall make of bronze.

**Exodus 27:4**

And you shall make for it a bronze grate of net-work, and you shall make for the grate four bronze rings on its four sides.

**Exodus 27:5**

And you shall set them under the grate of the altar below, and the grate shall reach to the middle of the altar.

**Exodus 27:6**

And you shall make poles for the altar of incorruptible wood, and you shall overlay them with bronze.

**Exodus 27:7**

And you shall bring the poles into the rings, and the poles shall be on the sides of the altar when it is carried.

**Exodus 27:8**

Hollow, of boards, you shall make it. According to what was shown you on the mountain, so you shall make it.

**Exodus 27:9**

And you shall make a court for the tent. On the side toward the south wind, hangings of woven fine linen, one hundred cubits long for one side.

**Exodus 27:10**

And their pillars twenty, and their twenty bronze bases, and their hooks and fastenings of silver.

**Exodus 27:11**

So also for the side toward the east wind, hangings one hundred cubits long, and their pillars twenty, and their twenty bronze bases, and the hooks and fastenings of the pillars silver-covered.

**Exodus 27:12**

And on the breadth of the court toward the sea, hangings fifty cubits, their pillars ten and their bases ten.

**Exodus 27:13**

And on the breadth of the court toward the south, hangings fifty cubits, their pillars ten and their bases ten.

**Exodus 27:14**

And the hangings shall be fifteen cubits high on one side, their pillars three and their bases three.

**Exodus 27:15**

And for the second side, fifteen cubits the height of the hangings, their pillars three and their bases three.

**Exodus 27:16**

And for the gate of the court, a screen twenty cubits long, of blue and purple and spun scarlet and woven fine linen, worked by an embroiderer, their pillars four and their bases four.

**Exodus 27:17**

All the pillars of the court all around silver-covered, their capitals silver, and their bases bronze.

**Exodus 27:18**

And the length of the court shall be one hundred by one hundred, and the breadth fifty by fifty, and the height five cubits, of woven fine linen, and their bases bronze.

**Exodus 27:19**

And all the furnishing and all the implements and the pegs of the court shall be bronze.

**Exodus 27:20**

And you shall charge the sons of Israel, and let them bring you pure beaten olive oil for the light, that a lamp may burn continually.

**Exodus 27:21**

In the tent of testimony, outside the curtain that is over the covenant, Aaron and his sons shall tend it from evening until morning before the Lord. This is an everlasting ordinance throughout your generations from the sons of Israel.

### Chapter 28

**Exodus 28:1**

And you, bring near to yourself Aaron your brother and his sons with him from among the sons of Israel to serve as priests to me: Aaron and Nadab and Abihu and Eleazar and Ithamar, Aaron's sons.

**Exodus 28:2**

And you shall make holy apparel for Aaron your brother, for honor and glory.

**Exodus 28:3**

And you shall speak to all the wise in mind, whom I have filled with a spirit of perception, and they shall make Aaron's holy apparel for the sanctuary, in which he shall serve as priest to me.

**Exodus 28:4**

And these are the garments they shall make: the breastpiece and the ephod and the robe and the fringed tunic and the turban and the sash. And they shall make holy garments for Aaron and his sons to serve as priests to me.

**Exodus 28:5**

And they shall take the gold and the blue and the purple and the scarlet and the fine linen.

**Exodus 28:6**

And they shall make the ephod of woven fine linen, work of an embroiderer.

**Exodus 28:7**

It shall have two shoulder-pieces joined together, fastened on the two sides.

**Exodus 28:8**

And the woven band of the shoulder-pieces that is upon it shall be of one piece with it, of gold and blue and purple and spun scarlet and woven fine linen.

**Exodus 28:9**

And you shall take the two emerald stones and engrave on them the names of the sons of Israel,

**Exodus 28:10**

six names on one stone and the remaining six names on the second stone, according to their births.

**Exodus 28:11**

As a stonecutter engraves a seal, so you shall engrave the two stones with the names of the sons of Israel.

**Exodus 28:12**

And you shall place the two stones on the shoulders of the ephod. They are memorial stones for the sons of Israel. And Aaron shall bear the names of the sons of Israel before the Lord on his two shoulders as a memorial concerning them.

**Exodus 28:13**

And you shall make settings of pure gold,

**Exodus 28:14**

and two braided cords of pure gold, mixed in floral work, woven. And you shall place the woven cords on the settings by their shoulder-pieces at the front.

**Exodus 28:15**

And you shall make a breastpiece of judgments, embroidered work. According to the pattern of the ephod you shall make it, of gold and blue and purple and spun scarlet and woven fine linen.

**Exodus 28:16**

It shall be square and doubled, a span its length and a span its breadth.

**Exodus 28:17**

And you shall weave into it a setting of four rows of stones. The first row: sardius, topaz, emerald.

**Exodus 28:18**

And the second row: carbuncle, sapphire, jasper.

**Exodus 28:19**

And the third row: ligure, agate, amethyst.

**Exodus 28:20**

And the fourth row: chrysolite, beryl, onyx, encircled and bound in gold according to their row.

**Exodus 28:21**

And the stones shall correspond to the names of the sons of Israel, twelve according to their names, engravings of seals, each according to its name for the twelve tribes.

**Exodus 28:22**

And you shall make on the breastpiece braided cords, chainwork of pure gold.

**Exodus 28:29**

And Aaron shall bear the names of the sons of Israel on the breastpiece of judgments upon his chest when he goes into the holy place, as a memorial before God.

**Exodus 28:30**

And you shall put on the breastpiece of judgments the Manifestation and the Truth, and it shall be on Aaron's chest when he goes into the holy place before the Lord. And Aaron shall bear the judgments of the sons of Israel upon his chest before the Lord continually.

**Exodus 28:31**

And you shall make the robe under the ephod all blue.

**Exodus 28:32**

And its opening shall be in the middle, with a woven border all around the opening, joined from the same work so that it may not tear.

**Exodus 28:33**

And on the hem of the robe below you shall make pomegranates like blossoming pomegranate fruit, of blue and purple and spun scarlet and woven fine linen, on the hem of the robe all around; and golden pomegranates and bells all around between them.

**Exodus 28:34**

A golden bell beside a pomegranate, and blossom-work on the hem of the robe all around.

**Exodus 28:35**

And Aaron shall wear it when he ministers, and its sound shall be heard when he goes into the holy place before the Lord and when he comes out, so that he may not die.

**Exodus 28:36**

And you shall make a pure gold plate, and engrave on it like the engraving of a seal, "Holiness of the Lord."

**Exodus 28:37**

And you shall place it on a blue cord, and it shall be on the turban; it shall be on the front of the turban.

**Exodus 28:38**

And it shall be on Aaron's forehead, and Aaron shall bear away the sins of the holy things, whatever holy gifts the sons of Israel sanctify. And it shall be on Aaron's forehead continually, acceptable for them before the Lord.

**Exodus 28:39**

And the fringes of the tunics shall be of fine linen, and you shall make a fine linen turban, and you shall make a sash, work of an embroiderer.

**Exodus 28:40**

And for Aaron's sons you shall make tunics and sashes, and you shall make turbans for them, for honor and glory.

**Exodus 28:41**

And you shall clothe Aaron your brother and his sons with him, and you shall anoint them and fill their hands and sanctify them, that they may serve as priests to me.

**Exodus 28:42**

And you shall make for them linen undergarments to cover the nakedness of their flesh; they shall reach from loins to thighs.

**Exodus 28:43**

And Aaron shall wear them, and his sons, whenever they enter the tent of testimony or approach the altar to minister in the holy place, so that they may not bring sin upon themselves and die. An everlasting statute for him and for his seed after him.

### Chapter 29

**Exodus 29:1**

And these are the things that you shall do for them to sanctify them, so that they may serve as priests to me: take one young bull from the herd and two flawless rams,

**Exodus 29:2**

and unleavened loaves kneaded with oil and unleavened cakes anointed with oil. You shall make them of fine wheat flour.

**Exodus 29:3**

And you shall put them on one basket, and you shall bring them on the basket, and the young bull and the two rams.

**Exodus 29:4**

And Aaron and his sons you shall bring to the doors of the tent of testimony, and you shall wash them with water.

**Exodus 29:5**

And taking the garments, you shall clothe Aaron your brother with the tunic and the robe and the ephod and the breastpiece, and you shall fasten the breastpiece to the ephod for him.

**Exodus 29:6**

And you shall put the turban on his head, and you shall put the holy plate on the turban.

**Exodus 29:7**

And you shall take the anointing oil and pour it on his head and anoint him.

**Exodus 29:8**

And you shall bring his sons and clothe them with tunics.

**Exodus 29:9**

And you shall gird them with sashes and put the turbans on them, and they shall have a priesthood for me forever. And you shall fill the hands of Aaron and the hands of his sons.

**Exodus 29:10**

And you shall bring the young bull to the doors of the tent of testimony, and Aaron and his sons shall lay their hands on the head of the young bull before the Lord by the doors of the tent of testimony.

**Exodus 29:11**

And you shall slaughter the young bull before the Lord by the doors of the tent of testimony.

**Exodus 29:12**

And you shall take some of the blood of the young bull and put it on the horns of the altar with your finger, and all the rest of the blood you shall pour out beside the base of the altar.

**Exodus 29:13**

And you shall take all the fat on the belly and the lobe of the liver and the two kidneys and the fat on them, and you shall place them on the altar.

**Exodus 29:14**

But the flesh of the young bull and its skin and its dung you shall burn with fire outside the camp, for it is a sin offering.

**Exodus 29:15**

And you shall take the one ram, and Aaron and his sons shall lay their hands on the head of the ram.

**Exodus 29:16**

And you shall slaughter it and, taking the blood, pour it around the altar.

**Exodus 29:17**

And you shall divide the ram by its limbs, and you shall wash the inward parts and the feet with water, and place them upon the divided pieces with the head.

**Exodus 29:18**

And you shall offer up the whole ram on the altar. It is a whole burnt offering to the Lord, a smell of sweet fragrance, an offering to the Lord.

**Exodus 29:19**

And you shall take the second ram, and Aaron and his sons shall lay their hands on the head of the ram.

**Exodus 29:20**

And you shall slaughter it and take some of its blood and place it on the tip of Aaron's right ear and on the thumb of his right hand and on the great toe of his right foot, and on the right earlobes of his sons and on the thumbs of their right hands and on the great toes of their right feet.

**Exodus 29:21**

And you shall take some of the blood from the altar and some of the anointing oil, and you shall sprinkle it on Aaron and on his garment and on his sons and on the garments of his sons with him, and he shall be sanctified, and his garment, and his sons, and the garments of his sons with him. And the blood of the ram you shall pour around the altar.

**Exodus 29:22**

And you shall take from the ram its fat and the fat covering the belly and the lobe of the liver and the two kidneys and the fat on them and the right arm, for this is an ordination.

**Exodus 29:23**

And one loaf of oil bread and one cake from the basket of unleavened bread set before the Lord.

**Exodus 29:24**

And you shall place all these on the hands of Aaron and on the hands of his sons, and you shall separate them as a separated offering before the Lord.

**Exodus 29:25**

And you shall take them from their hands and offer them on the altar upon the whole burnt offering, as a smell of sweet fragrance before the Lord. It is an offering to the Lord.

**Exodus 29:26**

And you shall take the breast from the ram of ordination that belongs to Aaron, and you shall separate it as a separated offering before the Lord, and it shall be your portion.

**Exodus 29:27**

And you shall sanctify the breast of the separated offering and the arm of the removal offering, that which was separated and that which was removed from the ram of ordination, from Aaron and from his sons.

**Exodus 29:28**

And it shall be for Aaron and for his sons an everlasting statute from the sons of Israel. For this is a removal offering, and it shall be a removal offering from the sons of Israel, from the sacrifices of salvation of the sons of Israel, a removal offering to the Lord.

**Exodus 29:29**

And the holy garments that are Aaron's shall belong to his sons after him, that they may be anointed in them and their hands filled in them.

**Exodus 29:30**

Seven days the priest who succeeds him from among his sons, who enters the tent of testimony to minister in the holy place, shall wear them.

**Exodus 29:31**

And you shall take the ram of ordination and boil its flesh in a holy place.

**Exodus 29:32**

And Aaron and his sons shall eat the flesh of the ram and the bread in the basket by the doors of the tent of testimony.

**Exodus 29:33**

They shall eat those things by which they were sanctified, to fill their hands, to sanctify them. But an outsider shall not eat of them, for they are holy.

**Exodus 29:34**

If any of the flesh of the sacrifice of ordination or of the bread remains until morning, you shall burn the remainder with fire. It shall not be eaten, for it is holy.

**Exodus 29:35**

And thus you shall do for Aaron and for his sons according to all that I commanded you. Seven days you shall fill their hands.

**Exodus 29:36**

And you shall offer the young bull of the sin offering on the day of purification, and you shall purify the altar when you sanctify it upon it, and you shall anoint it so as to sanctify it.

**Exodus 29:37**

Seven days you shall purify the altar and sanctify it, and the altar shall be most holy. Everyone touching the altar shall be sanctified.

**Exodus 29:38**

And these are the things that you shall offer upon the altar: two year-old flawless lambs each day upon the altar continually, a continual offering.

**Exodus 29:39**

The one lamb you shall offer in the morning, and the second lamb you shall offer at evening.

**Exodus 29:40**

And with the one lamb, a tenth of fine flour kneaded in a quarter-hin of beaten oil, and a drink offering, a quarter-hin of wine.

**Exodus 29:41**

And the second lamb you shall offer at evening. According to the morning sacrifice and according to its drink offering you shall offer it, as a smell of sweet fragrance, an offering to the Lord.

**Exodus 29:42**

A continual sacrifice throughout your generations at the doors of the tent of testimony before the Lord, where I will make myself known to you there so as to speak to you.

**Exodus 29:43**

And there I will appoint things for the sons of Israel, and I will be sanctified in my glory.

**Exodus 29:44**

And I will sanctify the tent of testimony and the altar, and Aaron and his sons I will sanctify to serve as priests to me.

**Exodus 29:45**

And I will be called upon among the sons of Israel, and I will be their God.

**Exodus 29:46**

And they shall know that I am the Lord their God, who brought them out of the land of Egypt to be called upon by them and to be their God.

### Chapter 30

**Exodus 30:1**

And you shall make an altar of incense of incorruptible wood, and you shall make it

**Exodus 30:2**

one cubit its length and one cubit its breadth. It shall be square, and its height two cubits. Its horns shall be from it.

**Exodus 30:3**

And you shall overlay it with pure gold, its top and its walls all around and its horns, and you shall make for it a twisted golden border all around.

**Exodus 30:4**

And you shall make two rings of pure gold under its twisted border. On its two sides you shall make them at the two corners, and they shall be holders for the poles, to carry it by them.

**Exodus 30:5**

And you shall make poles of incorruptible wood and overlay them with gold.

**Exodus 30:6**

And you shall place it opposite the curtain that is over the ark of testimonies, where I will make myself known to you there.

**Exodus 30:7**

And Aaron shall burn on it compounded fine incense every morning. When he trims the lamps, he shall burn incense on it.

**Exodus 30:8**

And when Aaron lights the lamps at evening, he shall burn incense on it, a continual incense before the Lord throughout their generations.

**Exodus 30:9**

And you shall not offer on it different incense, or an offering, or a sacrifice, and you shall not pour a drink offering on it.

**Exodus 30:10**

And Aaron shall make atonement upon its horns once each year. From the blood of purification for sins, from the atonement, once each year he shall cleanse it throughout their generations. It is Holy of Holies to the Lord.

**Exodus 30:11**

And the Lord spoke to Moses, saying,

**Exodus 30:12**

If you take the numbering of the sons of Israel at their visitation, each shall give a ransom for his life to the Lord, and there shall be no plague among them at their visitation.

**Exodus 30:13**

And this is what they shall give, all who pass through the visitation: half the didrachm, according to the didrachm of the sanctuary. Twenty obols to the didrachm. But the half-didrachm is an offering to the Lord.

**Exodus 30:14**

Everyone who passes through the visitation, from twenty years old and above, shall give the offering to the Lord.

**Exodus 30:15**

The rich shall not add and the poor shall not lessen from the half-didrachm in giving the offering to the Lord, to make atonement for your lives.

**Exodus 30:16**

And you shall take the silver of the offering from the sons of Israel and give it for the work of the tent of testimony, and it shall be a memorial before the Lord for the sons of Israel, to make atonement for your lives.

**Exodus 30:17**

And the Lord spoke to Moses, saying,

**Exodus 30:18**

Make a bronze laver and a bronze base for it for washing, and you shall place it between the tent of testimony and the altar, and you shall pour water into it.

**Exodus 30:19**

And Aaron and his sons shall wash from it their hands and feet with water.

**Exodus 30:20**

When they enter into the tent of testimony, they shall wash with water and not die; or when they approach the altar to minister and offer the whole burnt offerings to the Lord.

**Exodus 30:21**

They shall wash their hands and feet with water when they enter into the tent of testimony, so that they may not die. And it shall be an everlasting statute for them, for him and for his generations after him.

**Exodus 30:22**

And the Lord spoke to Moses, saying,

**Exodus 30:23**

And you, take sweet spices: five hundred shekels of choice-flowing myrrh, and half that amount, two hundred fifty of sweet cinnamon, and two hundred fifty of sweet calamus,

**Exodus 30:24**

and five hundred shekels of cassia by the sanctuary shekel, and a hin of olive oil.

**Exodus 30:25**

And you shall make it holy anointing oil, perfumed ointment, work of a perfumer. It shall be holy anointing oil.

**Exodus 30:26**

And you shall anoint with it the tent of testimony and the ark of testimony,

**Exodus 30:27**

and the lampstand and all its utensils and the altar of incense,

**Exodus 30:28**

and the altar of whole burnt offerings and all its utensils and the table and all its utensils and the laver and its base.

**Exodus 30:29**

And you shall sanctify them, and they shall be Holy of Holies. Everyone touching them shall be sanctified.

**Exodus 30:30**

And you shall anoint Aaron and his sons and sanctify them to serve as priests to me.

**Exodus 30:31**

And you shall speak to the sons of Israel, saying, "This shall be holy oil of anointing for you throughout your generations.

**Exodus 30:32**

It shall not be poured on human flesh, and according to this composition you shall not make any for yourselves in the same way. It is holy, and it shall be holiness for you.

**Exodus 30:33**

Whoever makes any like it, and whoever gives some of it to an outsider, shall be destroyed from his people."

**Exodus 30:34**

And the Lord said to Moses, "Take for yourself sweet spices: stacte, onycha, sweet galbanum, and pure frankincense. There shall be equal amounts of each.

**Exodus 30:35**

And they shall make from it incense, perfumed compound, work of a perfumer, mixed, pure, holy.

**Exodus 30:36**

And you shall beat some of it very fine and place it before the testimonies in the tent of testimony, where I will make myself known to you there. It shall be Holy of Holies for you.

**Exodus 30:37**

And incense according to this composition you shall not make for yourselves. It shall be a holy thing for you to the Lord.

**Exodus 30:38**

Whoever makes any like it to smell it shall perish from his people."

### Chapter 31

**Exodus 31:1**

And the Lord spoke to Moses, saying,

**Exodus 31:2**

"Look, I have called by name Bezalel son of Uri son of Hur, of the tribe of Judah.

**Exodus 31:3**

And I have filled him with a divine spirit of wisdom and understanding and knowledge in every work,

**Exodus 31:4**

to devise and design, to work gold and silver and bronze, and blue and purple and spun scarlet and woven fine linen,

**Exodus 31:5**

and in stone-cutting and woodwork, to work in every task.

**Exodus 31:6**

And I, behold, have appointed with him Oholiab son of Ahisamach, of the tribe of Dan. And to everyone wise in heart I have given understanding, and they shall make all that I commanded you:

**Exodus 31:7**

the tent of testimony and the ark of the covenant and the propitiatory upon it and the furnishings of the tent,

**Exodus 31:8**

and the altars and the table and all its utensils and the pure lampstand and all its utensils,

**Exodus 31:9**

and the laver and its base,

**Exodus 31:10**

and the ministering garments of Aaron and the garments of his sons to serve as priests to me,

**Exodus 31:11**

and the anointing oil and the compounded incense of the holy place. According to all that I commanded you, they shall make them."

**Exodus 31:12**

And the Lord spoke to Moses, saying,

**Exodus 31:13**

"And you, command the sons of Israel, saying, See that you keep my Sabbaths, for it is a sign between me and you throughout your generations, that you may know that I am the Lord who sanctifies you.

**Exodus 31:14**

And you shall keep the Sabbaths, because this is holy to the Lord for you. The one profaning it shall certainly be put to death. Everyone who does work on it, that life shall be destroyed from the midst of his people.

**Exodus 31:15**

Six days you shall do works, but on the seventh day is Sabbath rest, holy to the Lord. Everyone who does work on the seventh day shall certainly be put to death.

**Exodus 31:16**

And the sons of Israel shall keep the Sabbaths, to do them throughout their generations, an everlasting covenant.

**Exodus 31:17**

Between me and the sons of Israel it is an everlasting sign, because in six days the Lord made heaven and earth, and on the seventh day he ceased and rested."

**Exodus 31:18**

And when he finished speaking to him on Mount Sinai, he gave Moses the two tablets of testimony, stone tablets written with the finger of God.

### Chapter 32

**Exodus 32:1**

And when the people saw that Moses had delayed to come down from the mountain, the people gathered against Aaron and said to him, "Rise up and make gods for us who will go before us. For as for this Moses, the man who brought us up from Egypt, we do not know what has happened to him."

**Exodus 32:2**

And Aaron said to them, "Strip off the gold earrings in the ears of your wives and daughters and bring them to me."

**Exodus 32:3**

And all the people stripped off the gold earrings in their ears and brought them to Aaron.

**Exodus 32:4**

And he took them from their hands and formed them with the graving tool and made them a molten calf. And he said, "These are your gods, Israel, who brought you up from Egypt."

**Exodus 32:5**

And Aaron, seeing, built an altar before it, and Aaron proclaimed, saying, "Tomorrow is a feast of the Lord."

**Exodus 32:6**

And rising early the next day, he offered whole burnt offerings and brought sacrifices of salvation. And the people sat down to eat and drink and rose up to play.

**Exodus 32:7**

And the Lord spoke to Moses, saying, "Go, hurry down from here, for your people whom you brought up from Egypt have acted lawlessly.

**Exodus 32:8**

They have quickly turned aside from the way that I commanded them. They have made for themselves a calf and have worshiped it and sacrificed to it and said, 'These are your gods, Israel, who brought you up from Egypt.'"

**Exodus 32:10**

And now let me alone, and in anger I will destroy them, and I will make you a great nation.

**Exodus 32:11**

And Moses entreated before the Lord God and said, "Why, Lord, does angry wrath burn against your people whom you brought out from Egypt with great strength and with your high arm?

**Exodus 32:12**

Let not the Egyptians ever say, 'He brought them out with evil intent, to kill them in the mountains and consume them from the earth.' Stop from the anger of your wrath and be merciful concerning the evil of your people.

**Exodus 32:13**

Remember Abraham and Isaac and Jacob your servants, to whom you swore by yourself and spoke to them, saying, 'I will greatly multiply your seed like the stars of heaven in multitude, and all this land that I said I would give to your seed, and they shall possess it forever.'"

**Exodus 32:14**

And the Lord was propitiated concerning the evil that he had said he would do to his people.

**Exodus 32:15**

And Moses turned and went down from the mountain, and the two tablets of testimony were in his hands, stone tablets inscribed on both their sides, written on this side and that side.

**Exodus 32:16**

And the tablets were God's work, and the writing was God's writing, engraved upon the tablets.

**Exodus 32:17**

And Joshua, hearing the voice of the people crying out, said to Moses, "A sound of war is in the camp."

**Exodus 32:18**

And he said, "It is not the sound of those shouting in strength, nor the sound of those shouting in rout, but the sound of those striking up wine-song that I hear."

**Exodus 32:19**

And when he drew near to the camp, he saw the calf and the dances. And Moses, burning with anger, threw the two tablets from his hands and shattered them at the foot of the mountain.

**Exodus 32:20**

And taking the calf that they had made, he burned it in fire and ground it fine and scattered it on the water and made the sons of Israel drink it.

**Exodus 32:21**

And Moses said to Aaron, "What did this people do to you that you brought a great sin upon them?"

**Exodus 32:22**

And Aaron said to Moses, "Do not be angry, my lord. You know the rush of this people.

**Exodus 32:23**

For they say to me, 'Make gods for us who will go before us. For as for this Moses, the man who brought us up from Egypt, we do not know what has happened to him.'

**Exodus 32:24**

And I said to them, 'Whoever has gold ornaments, strip them off.' And they gave them to me, and I threw them into the fire, and out came this calf."

**Exodus 32:25**

And Moses, seeing that the people were scattered, for Aaron had scattered them into mockery for their enemies,

**Exodus 32:26**

stood at the gate of the camp and said, "Who is for the Lord? Let him come to me." Then all the sons of Levi gathered to him.

**Exodus 32:27**

And he said to them, "Thus says the Lord God of Israel: Let each put his sword upon his thigh and go through and return from gate to gate through the camp, and let each kill his brother and each his neighbor and each his nearest one."

**Exodus 32:28**

And the sons of Levi did as Moses spoke to them, and about three thousand men from the people fell that day.

**Exodus 32:29**

And Moses said to them, "You have filled your hands today to the Lord, each one against his son or his brother, so that blessing may be given upon you."

**Exodus 32:30**

And it came about on the next day that Moses said to the people, "You have sinned a great sin. And now I will go up to God, so that I may make atonement for your sin."

**Exodus 32:31**

And Moses returned to the Lord and said, "I beg, Lord, this people has sinned a great sin, and they made for themselves gods of gold.

**Exodus 32:32**

And now, if indeed you forgive their sin, forgive. But if not, blot me out from your book that you wrote."

**Exodus 32:33**

And the Lord said to Moses, "Whoever has sinned before me, him I will blot out from my book.

**Exodus 32:34**

And now go, descend, and lead this people to the place of which I spoke to you. Behold, my messenger goes before your face. But in the day when I visit, I will bring their sin upon them."

**Exodus 32:35**

And the Lord struck the people because of the making of the calf that Aaron made.

### Chapter 33

**Exodus 33:1**

And the Lord said to Moses, "Go, go up from here, you and your people whom you brought out of Egypt, to the land that I swore to Abraham and Isaac and Jacob, saying, 'To your seed I will give it.'

**Exodus 33:2**

And I will send my messenger with you and drive out the Amorite and Hittite and Perizzite and Girgashite and Hivite and Jebusite.

**Exodus 33:3**
