And I will bring you into a land flowing with milk and honey, but I will certainly not go up with you, because you are a stiff-necked people, lest I consume you on the way."

**Exodus 33:4**

And when the people heard this evil word, they mourned in mourning clothes.

**Exodus 33:5**

And the Lord said to the sons of Israel, "You are a stiff-necked people. See that I do not bring another plague upon you and consume you. Now then strip off your glorious garments and ornaments, and I will show you what I will do to you."

**Exodus 33:6**

So the sons of Israel stripped off their ornaments and their array from Mount Horeb onward.

**Exodus 33:7**

And Moses took his tent and pitched it outside the camp, far from the camp, and it was called Tent of Testimony. And everyone seeking the Lord went out to the tent outside the camp.

**Exodus 33:8**

And whenever Moses went into the tent outside the camp, all the people stood watching, each at the door of his tent, and they gazed after Moses until he entered the tent.

**Exodus 33:9**

And when Moses entered the tent, the pillar of the cloud came down and stood at the door of the tent, and he spoke with Moses.

**Exodus 33:10**

And all the people saw the pillar of the cloud standing at the door of the tent, and all the people stood and worshiped, each from the door of his tent.

**Exodus 33:11**

And the Lord spoke to Moses face to face, as if one would speak to his own friend. And he would return to the camp. But his servant Joshua son of Nun, a young man, would not depart from the tent.

**Exodus 33:12**

And Moses said to the Lord, "See, you say to me, 'Lead up this people,' but you have not made clear to me whom you will send with me. Yet you have said to me, 'I know you above all, and you have favor with me.'

**Exodus 33:13**

If then I have found favor before you, reveal yourself to me plainly, that I may see you, so that I may continue finding favor before you, and so that I may know that this great nation is your people."

**Exodus 33:14**

And he said, "I myself will go before you and give you rest."

**Exodus 33:15**

And he said to him, "If you yourself do not go with us, do not lead me up from here.

**Exodus 33:16**

For how shall it truly become known that both I and your people have found favor with you, except when you go with us? Then both I and your people will be glorified beyond all the nations that are on the earth."

**Exodus 33:17**

And the Lord said to Moses, "I will also do this word that you have spoken, for you have found favor before me, and I know you above all."

**Exodus 33:18**

And he said, "Show me your own glory."

**Exodus 33:19**

And he said, "I will pass before you in my glory, and I will call on my name, 'Lord,' before you. And I will have mercy on whom I have mercy, and I will pity whom I pity."

**Exodus 33:20**

And he said, "You cannot see my face, for no human shall see my face and live."

**Exodus 33:21**

And the Lord said, "Behold, a place is beside me. Stand upon the rock.

**Exodus 33:22**

And when my glory passes by, I will put you in a hollow of the rock and cover you with my hand until I pass by.

**Exodus 33:23**

And I will remove my hand, and then you shall see my back, but my face shall not be seen by you."

### Chapter 34

**Exodus 34:1**

And the Lord said to Moses, "Hew for yourself two stone tablets like the first, and come up to me on the mountain, and I will write on the tablets the words that were on the first tablets which you shattered.

**Exodus 34:2**

And be ready for the morning, and you shall come up to Mount Sinai and stand there for me on the top of the mountain.

**Exodus 34:3**

And let no one come up with you, nor be seen on all the mountain, and let not the sheep and the oxen graze near that mountain."

**Exodus 34:4**

And he hewed two stone tablets like the first. And Moses, rising early, went up to Mount Sinai, just as the Lord had appointed him, and Moses took the two stone tablets.

**Exodus 34:5**

And the Lord came down in a cloud and stood with him there and called on the name of the Lord.

**Exodus 34:6**

And the Lord passed before his face and proclaimed, "The Lord God, compassionate and merciful, slow to anger and very merciful and true,

**Exodus 34:7**

keeping righteousness and showing mercy to thousands, taking away lawlessness and unrighteousness and sins, and he will not clear the guilty, bringing fathers' lawlessness upon children and upon children's children, to the third and fourth generation."

**Exodus 34:8**

And Moses hurried, bowed to the earth, and worshiped.

**Exodus 34:9**

And he said, "If I have found favor before you, let my Lord go with us, for the people is stiff-necked, and you will take away our sins and our lawlessnesses, and we will be yours."

**Exodus 34:10**

And the Lord said to Moses, "Behold, I establish a covenant for you. Before all your people I will do glorious things that have not been done in all the earth or in any nation, and all the people among whom you are shall see the works of the Lord, because the things that I will do for you are wonderful.

**Exodus 34:11**

Pay attention to all that I command you. Behold, I drive out before you the Amorite and Canaanite and Hittite and Perizzite and Hivite and Girgashite and Jebusite.

**Exodus 34:12**

Pay attention to yourself, lest you make a covenant with those settled on the land into which you are entering, lest it become a stumbling block among you.

**Exodus 34:13**

You shall tear down their altars and break in pieces their pillars and cut down their groves, and the carved images of their gods you shall burn with fire.

**Exodus 34:14**

For you shall certainly not worship another god, for the Lord God, Jealous is his name; he is a jealous God.

**Exodus 34:15**

Lest you make a covenant with those settled on the land, and they prostitute themselves after their gods and sacrifice to their gods and invite you and you eat from their sacrifices,

**Exodus 34:16**

and you take some of their daughters for your sons and give some of your daughters to their sons, and your daughters prostitute themselves after their gods and make your sons prostitute themselves after their gods.

**Exodus 34:17**

And you shall not make molten gods for yourself.

**Exodus 34:18**

And you shall keep the feast of unleavened bread. Seven days you shall eat unleavened bread, as I commanded you, at the appointed time in the month of new grain, for in the month of new grain you came out from Egypt.

**Exodus 34:19**

Everything opening the womb is mine, every male firstborn of ox and every firstborn of sheep.

**Exodus 34:20**

And the firstborn of a beast of burden you shall redeem with a sheep. And if you do not redeem it, you shall pay a price. Every firstborn of your sons you shall redeem. You shall not appear before me empty.

**Exodus 34:21**

Six days you shall work, and on the seventh you shall rest; in seedtime and harvest you shall rest.

**Exodus 34:22**

And you shall keep for me the feast of weeks, the first of wheat harvest, and the feast of ingathering in the middle of the year.

**Exodus 34:23**

Three times in the year every male of yours shall appear before the Lord God of Israel.

**Exodus 34:24**

For when I cast out the nations before your face and enlarge your borders, no one shall desire your land when you go up to appear before the Lord your God three times in the year.

**Exodus 34:25**

You shall not slaughter the blood of my offerings with leaven, and the sacrifice of Passover shall not lie down until morning.

**Exodus 34:26**

The first produce of your land you shall place in the house of the Lord your God. You shall not offer a lamb in its mother's milk.

**Exodus 34:27**

And the Lord said to Moses, "Write these words for yourself, for on these words I have established a covenant with you and with Israel."

**Exodus 34:28**

And Moses was there before the Lord forty days and forty nights. He did not eat bread and did not drink water. And he wrote on the tablets the words of the covenant, the ten sayings.

**Exodus 34:29**

And when Moses came down from the mountain, the two tablets were in Moses' hands. And as he came down from the mountain, Moses did not know that the appearance of the skin of his face had been glorified when God spoke with him.

**Exodus 34:30**

And Aaron and all the elders of Israel saw Moses, and the appearance of the skin of his face had been glorified, and they feared to come near him.

**Exodus 34:31**

And Moses called them, and Aaron and all the rulers of the congregation turned back to him, and Moses spoke to them.

**Exodus 34:32**

And after these things all the sons of Israel came near to him, and he commanded them all that the Lord had spoken to him on Mount Sinai.

**Exodus 34:33**

And when he ceased speaking to them, he put a veil on his face.

**Exodus 34:34**

Whenever Moses went in before the Lord to speak with him, he removed the veil until he came out, and coming out he spoke to all the sons of Israel whatever the Lord had commanded him.

**Exodus 34:35**

And the sons of Israel saw the face of Moses, that it had been glorified, and Moses put the veil on his face until he went in to speak with him.

### Chapter 35

**Exodus 35:1**

And Moses gathered all the congregation of the sons of Israel and said to them, "These are the words that the Lord spoke, to do them.

**Exodus 35:2**

Six days you shall do works, but on the seventh day there shall be rest, holy Sabbath rest to the Lord. Everyone doing work on it shall die.

**Exodus 35:3**

You shall not burn fire in any of your dwellings on the day of the Sabbaths. I am the Lord."

**Exodus 35:4**

And Moses said to all the congregation of the sons of Israel, saying, "This is the word that the Lord commanded, saying,

**Exodus 35:5**

Take from yourselves a contribution to the Lord. Everyone accepting it in heart shall bring the Lord's first-contributions: gold, silver, bronze,

**Exodus 35:6**

blue, purple, doubled scarlet spun, woven fine linen, and goat hair,

**Exodus 35:7**

and rams' skins dyed red and blue skins and incorruptible wood,

**Exodus 35:9**

and sardius stones and stones for engraving, for the ephod and the robe.

**Exodus 35:10**

And every wise-hearted person among you, let him come and make all that the Lord has commanded:

**Exodus 35:11**

the tent and the hangings and the coverings and the rings and the bars and the posts,

**Exodus 35:12**

and the ark of testimony and its poles and its propitiatory and the curtain,

**Exodus 35:13**

and the table and all its utensils,

**Exodus 35:14**

and the lampstand of light and all its utensils,

**Exodus 35:16**

and the altar and all its utensils,

**Exodus 35:19**

and the holy garments of Aaron the priest and the garments in which they shall minister, and the tunics for Aaron's sons for the priesthood, and the anointing oil and the compounded incense."

**Exodus 35:20**

And all the congregation of the sons of Israel went out from Moses.

**Exodus 35:21**

And each one whose heart brought him, and each one to whom it seemed good in soul, brought the Lord's contribution for all the works of the tent of testimony and for all its services and for all the holy garments.

**Exodus 35:22**

And the men brought from among the women—everyone to whom it seemed good in mind—they brought seals and earrings and rings and necklaces and bracelets, every gold vessel. And all who brought gold contributions to the Lord brought them.

**Exodus 35:23**

And everyone with whom fine linen and blue skins and red-dyed rams' skins were found, brought them.

**Exodus 35:24**

And everyone offering a silver or bronze contribution brought the Lord's contributions, and those with whom incorruptible wood was found for all the works of preparation brought it.

**Exodus 35:25**

And every woman wise in mind spun with her hands and brought what was spun: the blue and the purple and the scarlet and the fine linen.

**Exodus 35:26**

And all the women whose mind moved them in wisdom spun the goat hair.

**Exodus 35:27**

And the rulers brought the sardius stones and the stones of filling for the ephod and for the breastpiece,

**Exodus 35:28**

and the spices and the oil of anointing and the compounded incense.

**Exodus 35:29**

Every man and woman whose mind moved them to come in and do all the works that the Lord had commanded to be done through Moses—the sons of Israel brought a contribution to the Lord.

**Exodus 35:30**

And Moses said to the sons of Israel, "Look, God has called by name Bezalel son of Uri son of Hur, of the tribe of Judah,

**Exodus 35:31**

and has filled him with a divine spirit of wisdom and understanding and knowledge of all things,

**Exodus 35:32**

to devise skillfully in all works of craftsmanship, to make gold and silver and bronze,

**Exodus 35:33**

and to cut stone and work wood and do every work of wisdom.

**Exodus 35:34**

And he gave improvement in understanding in his mind, both to him and to Oholiab son of Ahisamach, of the tribe of Dan.

**Exodus 35:35**

He filled them with wisdom, understanding, and insight to understand and do all the works of the holy place, and to weave woven and variegated work with the scarlet and fine linen, to do every work of craftsmanship and embroidery."

### Chapter 36

**Exodus 36:1**

And Bezalel and Oholiab and every wise-hearted one to whom wisdom and knowledge were given, to understand how to do all the works of the holy duties, did all that the Lord commanded.

**Exodus 36:2**

And Moses called Bezalel and Oholiab and all those having wisdom, to whom God gave knowledge in heart, and all who willingly wanted to come near to the works to perform them.

**Exodus 36:3**

And they received from Moses all the contributions that the sons of Israel had brought for all the works of the holy place to do them. And they were still receiving what was brought by those bringing it each morning.

**Exodus 36:4**

And all the wise men doing the works of the holy place came, each from his own work that he was doing,

**Exodus 36:5**

and they said to Moses, "The people are bringing more than enough for the works that the Lord commanded to be done."

**Exodus 36:6**

And Moses commanded, and proclamation was made in the camp, saying, "Let neither man nor woman work any longer for the first-contributions of the holy place." So the people were restrained from bringing more.

**Exodus 36:7**

And the works they had were sufficient for the making, and there was some left over.

**Exodus 36:8**

And every wise one among those working made the holy garments that belong to Aaron the priest, as the Lord commanded Moses.

**Exodus 36:9**

And they made the ephod of gold and blue and purple and spun scarlet and woven fine linen.

**Exodus 36:10**

And the plates of gold were cut into threads, so that they might be woven with the blue and the purple and the spun scarlet and the woven fine linen, a woven work.

**Exodus 36:11**

They made shoulder-pieces joined from both sides.

**Exodus 36:12**

A woven work intertwined into one another they made from the same material, of gold and blue and purple and spun scarlet and woven fine linen, just as the Lord commanded Moses.

**Exodus 36:13**

And they made the two emerald stones clasped and encircled in gold, engraved and carved like the carving of a seal, with the names of the sons of Israel.

**Exodus 36:14**

And he placed them on the shoulders of the ephod, as memorial stones of the sons of Israel, as the Lord commanded Moses.

**Exodus 36:15**

And they made the breastpiece, embroidered work, according to the work of the ephod, of gold and blue and purple and spun scarlet and woven fine linen.

**Exodus 36:16**

They made the breastpiece square and doubled, a span its length and a span its breadth, doubled.

**Exodus 36:17**

And a setting of four rows of stones was woven into it: sardius and topaz and emerald, the first row.

**Exodus 36:18**

And the second row: carbuncle and sapphire and jasper.

**Exodus 36:19**

And the third row: ligure and agate and amethyst.

**Exodus 36:20**

And the fourth row: chrysolite and beryl and onyx, encircled with gold and bound in gold.

**Exodus 36:21**

And the stones were twelve according to the names of the sons of Israel, engraved like seals, each with its own name for the twelve tribes.

**Exodus 36:22**

And they made on the breastpiece braided cords, woven chainwork of pure gold.

**Exodus 36:23**

And they made two gold settings and two gold rings and placed the two gold rings on both ends of the breastpiece.

**Exodus 36:24**

And they placed the gold woven cords on the rings at both sides of the breastpiece.

**Exodus 36:25**

And the two woven cords they set into the two couplings, and they placed them on the two settings, and they placed them on the shoulders of the ephod opposite each other at the front.

**Exodus 36:26**

And they made two gold rings and placed them on the two wings at the edge of the breastpiece, on the inner edge of the back of the ephod.

**Exodus 36:27**

And they made two gold rings and placed them on both shoulders of the ephod beneath it, at the front, by the joining above the woven band of the ephod.

**Exodus 36:28**

And he fastened the breastpiece from its rings to the rings of the ephod, joined with blue woven cord to the woven band of the ephod, so that the breastpiece might not loosen from the ephod, as the Lord commanded Moses.

**Exodus 36:29**

And they made the robe under the ephod, woven work, all blue.

**Exodus 36:30**

And the opening of the robe in the middle was woven close together, with a border around the opening, not to be torn.

**Exodus 36:31**

And on the hem of the robe below they made pomegranates like blossoming pomegranate fruit, of blue and purple and spun scarlet and woven fine linen.

**Exodus 36:32**

And they made gold bells and placed the bells on the hem of the robe all around between the pomegranates,

**Exodus 36:33**

a gold bell and a pomegranate on the hem of the robe all around, for ministering, as the Lord commanded Moses.

**Exodus 36:34**

And they made woven fine linen tunics for Aaron and his sons,

**Exodus 36:35**

and the turbans of fine linen and the headdress of fine linen and the undergarments of woven fine linen,

**Exodus 36:36**

and their sashes of fine linen and blue and purple and spun scarlet, embroidered work, as the Lord commanded Moses.

**Exodus 36:37**

And they made the gold plate, the separated thing of the holy place, of pure gold, and wrote on it engraved letters like the engraving of a seal: "Holiness to the Lord."

**Exodus 36:38**

And they placed on it a blue cord so that it would be on the turban above, as the Lord commanded Moses.

### Chapter 37

**Exodus 37:1**

And they made ten curtains for the tent.

**Exodus 37:2**

The length of one curtain was twenty-eight cubits; one measure belonged to all of them, and the breadth of one curtain was four cubits.

**Exodus 37:3**

And they made the curtain of blue and purple and spun scarlet and woven fine linen, woven work with cherubim.

**Exodus 37:4**

And they placed it on four incorruptible posts overlaid with gold, and their capitals gold, and their four bases silver.

**Exodus 37:5**

And they made the curtain of the door of the tent of testimony of blue and purple and spun scarlet and woven fine linen, woven work with cherubim.

**Exodus 37:6**

And its five posts and rings, and they overlaid their capitals and clasps with gold, and their five bases were bronze.

**Exodus 37:7**

And they made the court. Toward the south wind were hangings of the court of woven fine linen, one hundred by one hundred.

**Exodus 37:8**

And their pillars twenty and their bases twenty.

**Exodus 37:9**

And on the side toward the north, one hundred by one hundred, and their pillars twenty and their bases twenty.

**Exodus 37:10**

And on the side toward the sea, curtains of fifty cubits, their pillars ten and their bases ten.

**Exodus 37:11**

And on the side toward the east, fifty cubits.

**Exodus 37:12**

Hangings fifteen cubits at the back, and their pillars three and their bases three.

**Exodus 37:13**

And on the second back, on this side and that, by the gate of the court, hangings fifteen cubits, and their pillars three and their bases three.

**Exodus 37:14**

All the hangings of the court were of woven fine linen.

**Exodus 37:15**

And the bases of their pillars were bronze, and their hooks silver, and their capitals silver-covered, and all the pillars of the court were silver-covered.

**Exodus 37:16**

And the curtain of the gate of the court, embroidered work, of blue and purple and spun scarlet and woven fine linen, was twenty cubits long, and its height and breadth five cubits, equal to the hangings of the court.

**Exodus 37:17**

And their pillars four and their bases four bronze, and their hooks silver, and their capitals silver-covered.

**Exodus 37:18**

They also were silver-covered, and all the pegs of the court all around were bronze.

**Exodus 37:19**

And this was the ordering of the tent of testimony, as it was arranged by Moses for the service of the Levites through Ithamar son of Aaron the priest.

**Exodus 37:20**

And Bezalel son of Uri, of the tribe of Judah, made as the Lord commanded Moses.

**Exodus 37:21**

And Oholiab son of Ahisamach, of the tribe of Dan, was there, chief craftsman in woven works and embroidery and variegated weaving with scarlet and fine linen.

### Chapter 38

**Exodus 38:1**

And Bezalel made the ark,

**Exodus 38:2**

and overlaid it with pure gold inside and outside.

**Exodus 38:3**

And he cast for it four gold rings, two on one side and two on the second side,

**Exodus 38:4**

broad enough for the poles, so that they might carry it by them.

**Exodus 38:5**

And he made the propitiatory above the ark of pure gold,

**Exodus 38:6**

and the two gold cherubim,

**Exodus 38:7**

one cherub on one end of the propitiatory and one cherub on the second end of the propitiatory,

**Exodus 38:8**

overshadowing the propitiatory with their wings.

**Exodus 38:9**

And he made the presentation table of pure gold,

**Exodus 38:10**

and cast for it four rings, two on one side and two on the second side, broad enough so that it might be lifted with the poles in them.

**Exodus 38:11**

And he made the poles of the ark and of the table and overlaid them with gold.

**Exodus 38:12**

And he made the utensils of the table, both the dishes and the censers and the cups and the bowls in which he would pour offerings, of gold.

**Exodus 38:13**

And he made the lampstand that gives light, of solid gold, the shaft

**Exodus 38:14**

and the branches from both sides of it,

**Exodus 38:15**

and buds springing out from its branches, three on this side and three on that, matching one another.

**Exodus 38:16**

And as for the lamps that are at the ends, nut-like cups came from them, and sockets from them, so that the lamps might rest on them; and the seventh socket, at the top of the lampstand, at the summit above, was wholly solid gold.

**Exodus 38:17**

And on the lampstand were seven gold lamps, and its snuffers gold, and their trays gold.

**Exodus 38:18**

He silver-covered the posts and cast gold rings for each post and overlaid the bars with gold. He overlaid the posts of the curtain with gold and made gold hooks.

**Exodus 38:19**

He also made the rings of the tent gold, and the rings of the court, and rings for drawing out the upper covering, bronze.

**Exodus 38:20**

He cast the silver capitals of the tent and the bronze capitals of the door of the tent and the gate of the court, and he made silver hooks for the posts and silver-covered them on the posts.

**Exodus 38:21**

He made the pegs of the tent and the pegs of the court, bronze.

**Exodus 38:22**

He made the bronze altar from the bronze fire-pans that belonged to the men who stirred up revolt with the company of Korah.

**Exodus 38:23**

He made all the utensils of the altar and its fire-pan and the base and the bowls and the bronze flesh-hooks.

**Exodus 38:24**

He made for the altar a surrounding frame of net-work beneath its fire-pan underneath it as far as its middle, and he fixed to it four bronze rings on the four sides of the frame of the altar, wide enough for the bars, so that the altar might be carried by them.

**Exodus 38:25**

He made the holy anointing oil and the pure compounded incense, work of a perfumer.

**Exodus 38:26**

He made the bronze laver and its bronze base from the mirrors of the women who fasted, who fasted by the doors of the tent of testimony on the day it was set up.

**Exodus 38:27**

And he made the laver so that Moses and Aaron and his sons might wash their hands and feet from it. Whenever they entered the tent of testimony or approached the altar to minister, they washed from it, just as the Lord commanded Moses.

### Chapter 39

**Exodus 39:1**

All the gold that was worked for the works, for all the labor of the holy things, was gold from the contribution, twenty-nine talents and seven hundred twenty shekels by the holy shekel.

**Exodus 39:2**

And the silver contribution from the men counted in the congregation was one hundred talents and one thousand seven hundred seventy-five shekels,

**Exodus 39:3**

one drachma per head, half the shekel by the holy shekel, for everyone passing through the visitation, from twenty years and above, six hundred three thousand five hundred fifty.

**Exodus 39:4**

And the one hundred talents of silver became the casting of the one hundred capitals of the tent and the capitals of the curtain, one hundred capitals for the one hundred talents, a talent per capital.

**Exodus 39:5**

And the one thousand seven hundred seventy-five shekels they made into hooks for the posts, and he overlaid their capitals and adorned them.

**Exodus 39:6**

And the bronze of the contribution was seventy talents and one thousand five hundred shekels.

**Exodus 39:7**

And from it he made the bases of the door of the tent of testimony,

**Exodus 39:8**

and the bases of the court all around and the bases of the gate of the court and the pegs of the tent and the pegs of the court all around,

**Exodus 39:9**

and the bronze frame of the altar and all the utensils of the altar and all the instruments of the tent of testimony.

**Exodus 39:10**

And the sons of Israel did as the Lord commanded Moses; so they did.

**Exodus 39:11**

And from the gold that remained of the contribution they made vessels to minister with before the Lord.

**Exodus 39:12**

And the remaining blue and purple and scarlet they made into ministering garments for Aaron, so that he might minister with them in the holy place.

**Exodus 39:13**

And they brought the garments to Moses, and the tent and its utensils and its bases and its bars and the posts,

**Exodus 39:14**

and the ark of the covenant and its carrying-poles,

**Exodus 39:15**

and the altar and all its utensils and the anointing oil and the compounded incense,

**Exodus 39:16**

and the pure lampstand and its lamps, lamps for burning, and the oil of light,

**Exodus 39:17**

and the presentation table and all its utensils and the loaves laid out,

**Exodus 39:18**

and the holy garments that belong to Aaron and the garments of his sons for the priesthood,

**Exodus 39:19**

and the hangings of the court and the posts and the curtain of the door of the tent and of the gate of the court and all the utensils of the tent and all its instruments,

**Exodus 39:20**

and the red-dyed rams' skins and the blue skin coverings and the coverings for the remaining things,

**Exodus 39:21**

and the pegs and all the instruments for the works of the tent of testimony.

**Exodus 39:22**

As the Lord commanded Moses, so the sons of Israel made all the equipment.

**Exodus 39:23**

And Moses saw all the works, and they had done them just as the Lord commanded Moses. So they made them, and Moses blessed them.

### Chapter 40

**Exodus 40:1**

And the Lord spoke to Moses, saying,

**Exodus 40:2**

"On one day, the new moon of the first month, you shall set up the tent of testimony.

**Exodus 40:3**

And you shall place the ark of testimony and cover the ark with the curtain.

**Exodus 40:4**

And you shall bring in the table and arrange its arrangement, and you shall bring in the lampstand and place its lamps.

**Exodus 40:5**

And you shall place the gold altar for incense before the ark, and you shall place the curtain-screen on the door of the tent of testimony.

**Exodus 40:6**

And you shall place the altar of offerings by the doors of the tent of testimony.

**Exodus 40:8**

And you shall set the tent around it and sanctify all that belongs to it all around.

**Exodus 40:9**

And you shall take the anointing oil and anoint the tent and all that is in it, and you shall sanctify it and all its utensils, and it shall be holy.

**Exodus 40:10**

And you shall anoint the altar of offerings and all its utensils, and you shall sanctify the altar, and the altar shall be Holy of Holies.

**Exodus 40:12**

And you shall bring Aaron and his sons to the doors of the tent of testimony and wash them with water.

**Exodus 40:13**

And you shall clothe Aaron with the holy garments and anoint him and sanctify him, and he shall serve as priest to me.

**Exodus 40:14**

And you shall bring his sons and clothe them with tunics.

**Exodus 40:15**

And you shall anoint them just as you anointed their father, and they shall serve as priests to me. And it shall be for them an anointing of priesthood forever throughout their generations."

**Exodus 40:16**

And Moses did all that the Lord commanded him; so he did.

**Exodus 40:17**

And it came about in the first month, in the second year after they went out from Egypt, at the new moon, that the tent was set up.

**Exodus 40:18**

And Moses set up the tent and put on the capitals and inserted the bars and set up the posts.

**Exodus 40:19**

And he stretched out the curtains over the tent and put the covering of the tent above it, as the Lord commanded Moses.

**Exodus 40:20**

And taking the testimonies, he put them into the ark and placed the poles beside the ark.

**Exodus 40:21**

And he brought the ark into the tent and put on the curtain-covering and covered the ark of testimony, just as the Lord commanded Moses.

**Exodus 40:22**

And he placed the table in the tent of testimony on the north side of the tent, outside the curtain of the tent.

**Exodus 40:23**

And he set upon it the loaves of presentation before the Lord, just as the Lord commanded Moses.

**Exodus 40:24**

And he placed the lampstand in the tent of testimony on the south side of the tent.

**Exodus 40:25**

And he placed its lamps before the Lord, just as the Lord commanded Moses.

**Exodus 40:26**

And he placed the golden altar in the tent of testimony opposite the curtain.

**Exodus 40:27**

And he burned on it the compounded incense, just as the Lord commanded Moses.

**Exodus 40:29**

And he placed the altar of offerings by the doors of the tent.

**Exodus 40:33**

And he set up the court around the tent and the altar, and Moses finished all the works.

**Exodus 40:34**

And the cloud covered the tent of testimony, and the tent was filled with the glory of the Lord.

**Exodus 40:35**

And Moses was not able to enter into the tent of testimony, because the cloud overshadowed it, and the tent was filled with the glory of the Lord.

**Exodus 40:36**

And whenever the cloud went up from the tent, the sons of Israel set out with all their baggage.

**Exodus 40:37**

But if the cloud did not go up, they did not set out until the day the cloud went up.

**Exodus 40:38**

For cloud was upon the tent by day, and fire upon it by night, before all Israel in all their journeys."
